Club Atlético San Lorenzo de Almagro won the Inicial tournament in this round with the following line-ups:
Vélez Sarsfield: 13 Carlos Sebastián Sosa Silva; 2 Fernando Omar Tobio, 3 Emiliano Ramiro Papa, 
5 Fabián Alberto Cubero (captain, 32 Matías Pérez Acuña 69m), 6 Sebastián Enrique Domínguez (captain 69m), 
10 Federico Insúa (8 Héctor Miguel Canteros 67m), 12 Lucas David Pratto, 
21 Agustín Lionel Allione (booked 68m), 23 Leandro Luis Desábato (booked 51m, 19 Ezequiel Rescaldini 75m),
28 Ramiro Julián Cáseres, 29 Lucas Romero (booked 10m), Subs: 25 Alan Aguerre; 7 Jonathan Copete Valencia,
20 Brian Federico Ferreira, 30 Facundo Omar Cardozo. Coach: Ricardo Alberto Gareca Nardi.
San Lorenzo de Almagro: 12 Sebastián Alberto Torrico, 2 Mauro Darío Jesús Cetto, 
5 Juan Ignacio Mercier (captain 67m), 6 Santiago Juan Gentiletti, 7 Julio Alberto Buffarini,
10 Leandro Atilio Romagnoli (captain, 24 Juan Ignacio Cavallaro 67m), 11 Ángel Correa (8 Enzo Kalinski 82m),
14 Walter Kannemann (booked 9m), 20 Néstor Ezequiel Ortigoza, 
22 Alan Nahuel Ruiz (15 Héctor Daniel Villalba 71m), 28 Ignacio Piatti. Subs: 1 Cristian Darío Álvarez;
4 Gonzalo Sebastián Prósperi, 16 Fernando Elizari, 21 Emmanuel David Mas Sgros. 
Coach: Juan Antonio Pizzi Torroija.
Referees: Néstor Fabián Pitana; L1 Ariel Mariano Scime, L2 Gustavo Horacio Esquivel; 
4th Carlos Alberto Maglio.

Torneo del Interior 2014


A.Overview.
This tournament promoted 30 teams to Argentino B 2014/2015.
The rest of the teams came back to their regional leagues. 
It was ruled by the Consejo Federal [Federal Council].
It was played by:
a) The best teams of the regional leagues.
b) The relegated teams of Argentino B 2012/2013 tournament.
B.Qualification Stage:
There was 104 groups: 59 with 4 teams and 45 with 3 ones.
The two top teams of each group qualifyed to the First Phase.
The 32 best third position teams, in groups with 4 four teams, also progressed.
Head-to-head was used as main tie-breaker.
C.First, Second and Third Phases:
They were played in a straight knockout format with two legs.
If they tied in points and goals, PSO was used at the end of second leg.

1.Qualification Stage
2.First Phase
3.Second Phase
4.Third Phase
